<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

## Original request

Enforce browser scenario state restoration

Add a shared fixture boundary that restores executor, Git configuration, and lifecycle configuration even after failures, and waits for background cleanup before continuing.

## Why

Repair 1 successively identified execution-log contamination, lifecycle-file contamination, and a leaked synthetic origin remote. The existing repairs address these cases; a structural boundary would prevent recurrence.

## Verification

Run the affected scenarios individually, together, and in reversed order. Inject a scenario failure and verify that subsequent scenarios see their expected initial state.
